{"forms":[{"form":"metrics","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"What metric should be used for performance evaluation?","type":"example"},{"text":"What are the most important metrics to track for your business?","type":"example"},{"text":"It's the most important single metric that quantifies the predictive performance.","type":"example"},{"text":"How to measure marketing? Use these key metrics for measuring marketing effectiveness.","type":"example"},{"text":"There is a lack of standard metrics.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A way to measure something; a means of deriving a quantitative measurement or approximation for otherwise qualitative phenomena (especially used in engineering)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"measure"}]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"metric system"}],"glosses":["Ellipsis of metric system."],"tags":["abbreviation","alt-of","ellipsis"]},{"glosses":["A function which satisfies a particular set of formal conditions, created to generalize the notion of the distance between two points. Formally, a real-valued function d on M×M, where M is a set, is called a metric if (1) d(x,y)=0 if and only if x=y, (2) d(x,y)=d(y,x) for all pairs (x,y), and (3) d obeys the triangle inequality."],"synonyms":[{"word":"distance function"}],"topics":["mathematical-analysis","mathematics","sciences"]},{"glosses":["A metric tensor."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈmɛt.ɹɪk/"}],"upstream_line":5811,"upstream_offset":35352245,"upstream_record_sha256":"67b8b8bd4a0cf5f1f5f971ca97f6afc91b7ff0477dccc1c38d41ffbdde15157a","word":"metric"}
